2026-11-20T17:34:06.4319773Z [36;1m# and to require a codes/ or circuits/ change, which was two entries[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4320194Z [36;1m# short of what the scope check lets a submission carry. So a PR[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4320531Z [36;1m# that added a certificate, beside its entry or alone, ran every[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4320886Z [36;1m# self-test: about an hour of CI per PR, for a suite in which a[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4321237Z [36;1m# few tests open anything under certs/. The certificate checker is[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4321586Z [36;1m# not among the things skipped. It is its own step below and runs[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4323612Z [36;1m# entry.[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4326315Z [36;1m# cost: #2901 tightened codes/646-162-26.json while[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4327950Z [36;1m# right there in the test source, so ask the suite which files[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4329434Z [36;1m# the files it can name are this tree's own tests, filtered to[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4329730Z [36;1m# that shape below before anything reaches pytest.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4329954Z [36;1m#[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4330535Z [36;1m# (certs/<slug>.json, certs/heuristic/<slug>.json, repro/<slug>.json),[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4330875Z [36;1m# and a test can pin what one of them says, so their slugs are read[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4331153Z [36;1m# the same way.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4331542Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4331826Z [36;1m# The shape filter used to admit only a test directly under one of[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4332231Z [36;1m# the four directories. research/ has since grown subdirectories[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4332650Z [36;1m# (audits/, certs/, cost/, local2d/, provenance/) that hold 17 test[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4334127Z [36;1m#[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4335222Z [36;1m# of its published reproductions under $GATE_GOLDENS, named for the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4335629Z [36;1m# entry, and verify/test_validation_gate.py fails when[0m

2026-11-20T17:34:06.4336769Z [36;1m# the test's source, so the grep cannot see them: #5929 changed the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4337155Z [36;1m# circuit tier of codes/160-36-6.json, passed here, and the gate[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4337537Z [36;1m# test then failed on every pull request that ran the suite until[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4337939Z [36;1m# #6241 re-recorded the golden. A changed entry that has a golden[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4338316Z [36;1m# therefore selects the gate's test file as well.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4338601Z [36;1mslug_tests=""[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4339150Z [36;1mslugs=$(git diff --name-status --diff-filter=AMDRT "$base"...HEAD -- codes circuits certs repro \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4339587Z [36;1m          | cut -f2- | tr '\t' '\n' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4339943Z [36;1m          | sed -n -e 's|^codes/\(.*\)\.json$|\1|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4340269Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^circuits/\([^/]*\)/.*|\1|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4340597Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^certs/\(.*/\)\{0,1\}\([^/]*\)\.json$|\2|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4341065Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^repro/\([^/]*\)\.json$|\1|p' | sort -u)[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4341354Z [36;1mfor slug in $slugs; do[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4341709Z [36;1m  # Both spellings of one entry: the slug the file is named for,[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4342104Z [36;1m  # and the [[n,k,d]] form a test may cite instead. -F keeps a[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4342477Z [36;1m  # slug that looks like a pattern (and one that starts with a[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4342808Z [36;1m  # dash) from meaning anything to grep.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4343150Z [36;1m  slug_tests="$slug_tests $(grep -rlF --include='test_*.py' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4343528Z [36;1m                -e "$slug" -e "${slug//-/,}" \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4343883Z [36;1m                research verify site cli || true)"[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4344244Z [36;1m  if [ -f "$GATE_GOLDENS/$slug.json" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4344623Z [36;1m    slug_tests="$slug_tests verify/test_validation_gate.py"[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4344926Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4345134Z [36;1mdone[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4345374Z [36;1mslug_tests=$(printf '%s\n' $slug_tests \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4345797Z [36;1m               | grep -E '^(research|verify|site|cli)/([A-Za-z0-9_]+/)*test_[A-Za-z0-9_.]+\.py$' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4346216Z [36;1m               | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/ *$//' || true)[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4346512Z [36;1mif [ -n "$slug_tests" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4346942Z [36;1m  echo "self-tests naming an entry this PR changed: $slug_tests" | tee -a "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4347310Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4347606Z [36;1m# A circuit.ler claim nothing in the PR touched can only go stale[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4348010Z [36;1m# through the stack that measures it: the measured-rate tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4348431Z [36;1m# decoder, the pinned dependencies (a stim bump in uv.lock) or the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4349061Z [36;1m# accelerator build, which is the LER_STACK list in this job's env.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4349469Z [36;1m# A PR that leaves all of those alone gets the --ler-base run; a PR[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4349853Z [36;1m# that touches any of them re-measures a sample of the claims[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4350242Z [36;1m# before it merges, and the weekly runs re-measure them all (the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4350681Z [36;1m# sweep that passes --ler-sample below has the argument). Everything else[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4351084Z [36;1m# under verify/ (the structural verifier, the circuit tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4351450Z [36;1m# gate, the policy checkers, tests, fixtures, the integrity[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4351880Z [36;1m# manifest) runs on every entry in the --ler-base pass as well and[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4352251Z [36;1m# cannot change a measured rate, so it is left out. The list used[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4352637Z [36;1m# to be all of verify/ minus tests, checkers, and fixtures: a[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4353022Z [36;1m# prose-checker edit scheduled the full re-measure on PR #3418, and[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4353442Z [36;1m# once those were excluded a schema-check edit did on #4653 and[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4353820Z [36;1m# #4682, which with about an hour of self-tests runs past this[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4354179Z [36;1m# job's 130-minute timeout. A diff that cannot be computed exits[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4354600Z [36;1m# non-zero and so counts as changed (fail closed). $LER_STACK is[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4354968Z [36;1m# unquoted so that it splits into one pathspec per entry.[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4355332Z [36;1mif git diff --quiet "$base"...HEAD -- $LER_STACK; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4355686Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=false[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4355926Z [36;1melse[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4356156Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=true[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4356398Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4356708Z [36;1mecho "codes_changed=$codes_changed" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
2026-11-20T17:34:06.4357115Z [36;1mecho "data_only=$data_only" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
